## 🤔 O que é o Seedance 2.0?

**Seedance 2.0** é um modelo de geração de vídeo desenvolvido pela **ByteDance**. É o primeiro modelo do setor que suporta **entrada simultânea de quatro modalidades** — imagem, vídeo, áudio e texto.

**Principais Recursos:**

* 🎥 **Texto para Vídeo** — Gere vídeos a partir de descrições textuais
* 🖼️ **Imagem para Vídeo** — Anime imagens estáticas em vídeos dinâmicos
* 📹 **Vídeo para Vídeo** — Transforme e estenda vídeos existentes
* 🎵 **Áudio como Base** — Gere vídeos a partir de entrada de áudio
* 📐 **Resolução até 1080p**, duração de 4–15 segundos
* 🔊 **Dublagem e trilha automáticas** — Narração e música de fundo automáticas

### KAELDRAX [@Pixie_Create_a_premi] — ASHES OF THE FIRST THRONE

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
KAELDRAX [@Pixie_Create_a_premi] — ASHES OF THE FIRST THRONE
Duration: 15 Seconds
Use storyboard [@Pixie_ConceptLong_be] as reference only.
Create one seamless cinematic boss-introduction sequence.
Do not render storyboard panels.
Do not render text.
Do not render annotations.
Do not render page layouts.
MASTER PROMPT
Generate a AAA dark fantasy final-boss cinematic featuring Kaeldrax, The Binded King.
A fallen demi-god monarch with colossal horns, ash-gray skin, cursed ember eye, tattered royal robes, immense beast tail wrapped in ancient bindings, and a molten golden greatsword.
The environment is an ancient underground throne kingdom buried beneath the world. Cathedral-sized halls, broken chains, volcanic ash storms, rivers of molten gold, and colossal ruined monuments surround him.
The sequence should feel like:
Elden Ring remembrance boss
Morgott introduction
Godfrey phase transition
Dark Souls final boss
Diablo IV cinematic
Mythological apocalypse
TIMELINE
0.0–2.0s
Extreme wide aerial shot.
Ancient underground kingdom beneath mountains.
Ash drifting through the air.
Molten rivers illuminate colossal architecture.
Camera descends toward throne chamber.
2.0–3.5s
Massive throne room.
Thousands of broken chains hang from cathedral pillars.
Kaeldrax sits motionless on the Ashen Throne.
The room feels abandoned by gods.
3.5–5.0s
Close-up.
His cursed eye opens.
A single ember ignites.
Golden fire spreads beneath cracked skin.
Ancient power awakens.
5.0–6.5s
Kaeldrax rises slowly.
Chains fall from body.
Heavy impacts echo through chamber.
Camera circles dramatically.
His silhouette dominates frame.
6.5–8.0s
Greatsword drags across stone.
Molten sparks trail behind.
Ancient runes awaken across blade.
Golden fire spreads along weapon.
8.0–10.0s
Entire throne room begins collapsing.
Pillars crack.
Ash vortex expands.
Molten light floods the environment.
The kingdom reacts to its king's return.
10.0–12.0s
Kaeldrax unleashes a colossal sword swing.
A wave of ember-fire erupts across the arena.
Ground fractures.
Ancient monuments shatter.
12.0–13.5s
His beast tail ignites.
Golden fire spirals around him.
Half of his face becomes consumed by divine ember corruption.
Ancient fury awakens.
13.5–15.0s
Final remembrance-boss reveal.
Camera rapidly pulls back.
Kaeldrax stands atop the shattered throne.
A gigantic burning eclipse forms behind him.
Ash storm fills the sky.
Greatsword glowing.
The last king of a dead age.
Fade to black.
STYLE BLOCK
Elden Ring cinematic, FromSoftware boss introduction, dark fantasy masterpiece, painterly realism, volcanic atmosphere, drifting ash, molten gold illumination, colossal scale, gothic architecture, mythological tragedy, divine corruption, dramatic silhouettes, cinematic fog, ember particles, AAA game trailer quality.
SEEDANCE NEGATIVE PROMPT
text, subtitles, captions, panel numbers, storyboard borders, timestamps, camera notes, arrows, production annotations, UI overlays, watermarks, comic panels, page layouts, split screen, modern technology, sci-fi elements, futuristic weapons, anime cel shading, photorealistic live action, low detail textures, weak particles, character redesign, face drift, extra limbs, duplicate character, costume drift, low-scale environments, static poses.
```

### PART 1 (0:00–0:15)

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
PART 1 (0:00–0:15)
Cut 1 (0:00–0:05) — Sneaking Through the Market

1920s Osaka market, Betamax film look. Yuki stealthily tiptoes through crowded stalls, sleeve over her mouth, eyes scanning. She spots a ramen shop, grins, and dashes through the noren curtain.

Cut 2 (0:05–0:10) — Ramen at Last

Inside the steamy ramen shop, Yuki excitedly orders a giant tonkotsu ramen with extra pork and egg. She sits down as the steaming bowl arrives, smiling and raising her chopsticks like a sword.

Cut 3 (0:10–0:15) — Ninja Ambush

Three burly ninja-like thugs notice her, pull on masks, and suddenly attack. Tables scatter as they leap through the air. Yuki flips clear while protecting her ramen, frozen mid-slurp with an annoyed glare.

PART 2 (0:15–0:30)

Cut 4 (0:15–0:20) — Noodle Defense

A ninja throws a shuriken. Yuki instantly catches it by stretching a single ramen noodle between her chopsticks, then effortlessly dodges the ninjas' attacks with playful martial-arts agility.

Cut 5 (0:20–0:25) — Men-no-Jutsu

Yuki plants her feet and chants, "Men-no-jutsu!" Glowing ramen noodles rise from the bowl and whip through the shop, wrapping around all three ninjas and tying them together.

Cut 6 (0:25–0:30) — Bowl Finish

The tangled ninjas stumble and crash into a heap. Yuki leaps high into the air and smashes the empty ramen bowl onto their heads with a triple bonk. Freeze frame on her fierce pout as her gold-and-red hairpins sparkle under the lantern light.
```

### Create a realistic GRWM (Get Ready With Me) lifestyle video featuring a stylish…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a realistic GRWM (Get Ready With Me) lifestyle video featuring a stylish Korean woman with long silky black hair, radiant skin, and a fresh natural look. Bright modern apartment with floor-to-ceiling windows, warm morning sunlight, Scandinavian-inspired décor, premium fashion vlog aesthetic, cinematic handheld camera, ultra-realistic photography.

The video opens with her sitting on the edge of her bed. She smiles softly, stretches, and walks to the window, pulling the curtains open as warm golden sunlight fills the room. She makes her bed before heading to her vanity.

She begins a refreshing skincare routine using a cleanser, toner, serum, moisturizer, and lip balm. Close-up beauty shots highlight glowing skin while natural sunlight reflects through the room.

Next, she styles her hair into soft loose waves using a curling iron, gently brushing through the curls for a smooth finish. She applies light everyday makeup including foundation, blush, mascara, soft eyeliner, and a glossy pink lip tint, smiling naturally into the mirror.

She walks to her wardrobe, slides open the closet, and compares several outfits before choosing a pastel pink fitted crop top with high-waisted blue jeans. She changes into the outfit, adjusts the fit, and adds small gold hoop earrings, a delicate necklace, a watch, and sunglasses.

She sprays her favorite perfume, slips on white sneakers, picks up a small shoulder bag, and grabs an iced coffee from the kitchen counter.

Standing in front of a full-length mirror, she checks her outfit from different angles, spins once with a smile, and takes a quick mirror selfie.

The video ends as she opens the apartment door and walks outside into the bright morning sunshine. The camera follows her as she confidently strolls down a peaceful tree-lined street holding her iced coffee, ending with a cinematic pullback.

Style: premium GRWM fashion vlog, luxury lifestyle content, warm natural lighting, cozy apartment aesthetic, realistic handheld and gimbal cinematography, soft cinematic color grading, photorealistic, elegant transitions, shallow depth of field, 4K HDR, 16:9 widescreen, no subtitles, no text overlays.
```

A 15-second cinematic underwater documentary transitioning through all 6 storyboard panels in strict order. Render as seamless full-screen cinematic shots only. Ignore grid layout.

Shot 6 (12.5-15.0s): A majestic finale. A lone diver stands on a rocky ledge at the bottom of a deep canyon, looking up at a sky filled with floating jellyfish and bioluminescent corals. Camera slowly pulls back. ZERO TEXT.

Style reinforcement: cinematic lighting, realistic water physics, seamless transitions, NO text, NO grid, NO numbers, consistent artistic style throughout.

### HAPPY 4TH!

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
HAPPY 4TH!

Bookmark kick things off with a bang!

Use the attached production board as the exact 14-second scene plan. Generate the highest-quality cinematic Seedance 2 sequence from it. Preserve shot order, characters, costumes, location, dialogue, and timing. Improve realism, motion, lighting, and image clarity.

STYLE: 1980s $500M action film. Real 35mm anamorphic, 2.39:1, Kodak 5219 pushed one stop, organic grain, gate weave, halation, wet-pavement reflections, practical pyrotechnics, no CGI, no plastic skin, no anime, no cartoon.

CHARACTERS:
Ninja: masked male, black hooded gi, sharp eyes, twin swords, athletic.
Blonde: petite 24yo LA, wet wavy blonde hair, white tied crop top, denim cutoffs, sneakers, intense.
Robots: chrome humanoids, red sensor eyes, reflective metal.

GLOBAL: 24fps, cinematic motion blur, no jitter, no morphing, no identity drift, no costume drift, no robot drift. Faces sharp in close-ups. Sparks/smoke/fireworks enhance, never hide action.

1A 00: 00–00:02 WIDE / SURROUNDED. Static low 25mm push-in. Robots advance from all sides. Ninja reaches sword, blonde raises fists. BLONDE: “Guess the party found us.”

1B 00: 02–00:04 MED WIDE / FIRST STRIKE. Track left 50mm. Blonde kicks robot R-L, sparks. Ninja lunges L-R with sword. NINJA: “Stay moving.”

No dialogue. ONE continuous shot, no cuts.

SCENE
Frozen post-apocalyptic wasteland. @truck1 races down a snow-packed highway at full power, a massive blizzard wall chasing directly behind. @monst erupts from the storm behind the truck, plunges its clawed hand into the ground beneath it and heaves it upward — the truck launches, tumbles, lands and rolls to rest upside down.

REFERENCES
@truck1: armored snow-truck — welded steel cab, riveted armor, roof rack of amber lamps, steel snowplow blade, oversized studded front tires, studded rear snow-tracks, exhaust stacks, cargo box. Ice-crusted teal-grey steel, burnt orange rust accents. 100% matches reference.
@monst: towering humanoid blizzard monster of compacted snow and translucent ice — frost-crystal surfaces, jagged icicles, pale amber-glowing eyes, maw of icicle teeth, clawed hands of fused ice shards, edges half-dissolved into drifting painterly snow. 100% matches reference.
Cab: male driver, screen-left seat, both hands on the wheel; female passenger, screen-right, hands braced on dash, never touching the wheel. Scavenged fur-and-scrap cold-weather gear. All elements share the same gouache rendering — no style drift between truck, monster, characters, environment.

BLOCKING
Frame one: side tracking view, truck already at full speed left-to-right, mid-frame, snow rooster-tails as flowing brushwork, blizzard wall filling background left. Storm and @monst always behind the truck; road ahead (screen-right) clear.

CAMERA
One weighted continuous move: fast side track at windshield height, both characters readable through glass → ease back and low as the snow-bulge gains, letting the truck pull ahead so the eruption reads full-height → decelerate, holding the airborne truck against the storm through its rotations → drop to just above the snow, settle static as the truck crashes, rolls toward the lens, stops upside down filling frame. Smooth hand-crafted animation feel, no jitter. One speed-ramp into slow motion at the scoop, ramp back after.

ACTION
0–4s: Full-speed chase past frozen wrecks, plow bursting through a drift in painterly powder, suspension lurching, exhaust steam shearing back in soft strokes. Driver white-knuckled, eyes to mirror; passenger snaps her head toward the storm.
4–7s: A fast bulge of churning snow races under the road behind the truck, gaining — @monst erupts upward in a tower of gouache powder, amber eyes igniting, icicle maw opening. Snow cascades off it with real weight.
7–10.5s: Slow motion — the clawed hand plunges into the ground under the truck, heaving a wave of frozen earth that levers it airborne. Full vehicle mass: two heavy, slightly sluggish rotations, glass and debris in slow painterly arcs, both characters thrown against belts, faces snapping to terror.
10.5–15s: Real time, camera static above the snow. The truck drops, hits in a blooming powder impact, rolls toward the lens with decelerating momentum, rests upside down filling frame. One front wheel spins freely, slows, stops. @monst looms as a half-dissolved silhouette in the haze behind, amber eyes glowing softly. Hold.

PHYSICS
True mass, never floaty. Tires bite and shed packed snow; the submerged monster displaces a wake-like bulge. ALL wheels and tracks stay mounted in position through the flip, spinning but never detaching. Heavy front strikes first on landing, roof crumples, chassis rolls with real friction. Bodies obey inertia; fur and scarves whip.

LIGHT
Cold overcast day; hidden warm sun backlights the storm so @monst reads as a brighter churning silhouette. Amber truck lamps bloom warm through volumetric painterly haze — the strongest warm accents in frame.

AUDIO
No music. Engine roar, track clatter, blizzard wind, sub-bass rumble of the bulge, powder eruption, ice-cracking roar, groaning metal, landing impact, wheel ticking to a stop.

LOCKS
No photorealism, 3D CGI, hard outlines, cel shading, style drift. One shot, single speed-ramp only. Storm and @monst never ahead of the truck. Flip comes from the ground-scoop — claws never strike the body. Woman never touches the wheel. Wheels never detach. Travel left-to-right, locked.

Final location is the circuit after sunset with empty grandstands and a quiet pit lane. No real-world team logos, no copyrighted liveries, no recognizable branding.

00:10–00:12

00:12–00:15
After the race, the circuit is mostly empty. He sits quietly on the pit wall holding his helmet beside him, watching fading light over the track. Wind lightly moves his hair. He notices the camera, gives a small tired smile, then looks back at the circuit.

Audio: Natural ambient sound only — engine revs, tire squeal, radio chatter, wheel guns, crew communication, crowd roar, rain spray on asphalt, distant announcements, metallic garage sounds, subtle evening wind. No music. No sound design. No narration.

Goal: Authentic elite motorsport life captured like a forgotten home video from the early 2000s — candid, imperfect, realistic, intense, and deeply believable.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
添付画像のキャラクターを参照。キャラクターの顔立ち、髪型、体格、衣装、色、雰囲気を忠実に再現する。

10秒、横長16:9、映画的アニメーション映像。総制作費10億。舞台は薄暗く無機質な巨大空間。コンクリート、金属壁、冷たい床、わずかな霧、背景は静かで広く、無駄な装飾はない。

【武器指定】
武器タイプ：大鎌
素材、装飾：紫と白の悪魔的装飾
発光色：紫の炎
召喚演出：足元の影が割れ、紫の粒子が三日月型の刃の形を作って実体化する
構え方：片手で持ち、肩に担いで静止する。

キャラクターは画面奥から静かに歩いてくる。足音だけが響く。表情は冷静で鋭く、感情を抑えている。俯瞰で撮影した後に、歩きながら低い声で呟く口元を映す。
「○○○」

その瞬間、キャラクターの足元の何もない空間に淡いエネルギーの光が発生する。床から粒子状の光がゆっくり立ち上がり、線、輪郭、刃、柄、装飾の順に武器の形を形成していく。最初は半透明の光の輪郭、次第に金属質な実体へ変化する。光はキャラクターの歩みに合わせて揺れ、武器の形が徐々に明確になる。カメラは形成中の武器を追跡。

カメラはキャラクターの周囲を激しく回り込む。ローアングルで足元の光を映し、次に武器の輪郭へ急接近し、さらにキャラクターの横顔、鋭い目、揺れる衣装、手元を素早く切り替えてフォーカスする。手持ちカメラ風の揺れ、スピード感のある回り込み、浅い被写界深度。背景は流れるようにブラーし、キャラクターと武器だけが鮮明に浮かび上がる。

武器が完全に実体化した瞬間、強い光が一瞬だけ空間を照らす。キャラクターは歩みを止めず、自然な動作でその武器を手に取る。金属の重みが伝わるように、手にした瞬間にわずかに腕が沈む。光の粒子が散り、武器の表面に最後のエネルギーラインが走る。

ラスト、キャラクターは画面中央で静止し、武器を構える。カメラは正面からゆっくり寄り、顔と武器を同時に広く捉える。空間は静まり返る。キャラクターは小さく、だるそうな声で呟く。
「○○○」

高品質、シネマティック、緊張感、無機質、静寂、エネルギー召喚、激しいカメラワーク、キャラクター崩れなし、武器の形状破綻なし、顔の一貫性維持。
```

Calm expression, highly focused eyes

SETTING

Ancient dense forest battlefield

0–3s

Enemy patrol footsteps approach through fog.

She slowly raises her bow.

3–6s

12–15s

### Two reference images: The woman in [image1] and the cart in [image2]. Maintain…

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Two reference images: The woman in [image1] and the cart in [image2]. Maintain her exact identity throughout — same face, same dark hair in a loose messy low bun with escaping strands, same worn oversized faded burgundy hoodie over a faded off-white t-shirt, same dark charcoal track pants and scuffed sneakers, same work gloves, same thin red string bracelet. The cart is identical to [image2] in every scene: a tall two-wheeled welded metal frame carrying one huge white woven sack half full of collected plastic bottles, with two long pull handles at the front.

How she moves with the cart: she stands between the two handles with her back to the cart, grips one handle in each hand at her sides, leans forward and walks — the cart rolls behind her on its two wheels, like a rickshaw puller. This is how she travels in every walking scene.

Character: Young Turkish woman, 22, a street waste collector in Istanbul. Breathtakingly beautiful but completely indifferent to it — focused, hardworking, dignified, reserved. Natural believable behavior throughout; she works as if no one is watching.

Location: A narrow back street in a working-class Istanbul neighborhood (kenar mahalle) on a bright sunny morning. Golden morning sunlight fills the street from the very first frame. Worn concrete and plastered apartment buildings, 4-5 stories, in faded pastel colors — some unpainted, patched, weathered — with rusted balcony railings, laundry hanging between windows, tangled electrical cables overhead, satellite dishes, air conditioner units on facades, a few old parked cars, cracked asphalt and patched pavement. Grey galvanized metal municipal dumpsters with curved lids holding tied black garbage bags of ordinary household waste. Her white sack is where the collected plastic bottles accumulate. Crowded, lived-in, authentic — not renovated, not touristic.

Visual Style: Ultra-realistic documentary realism. Genuine candid behavior. Natural body language. Unscripted slice-of-life feeling. Strong environmental authenticity. Rich real-world details and believable human motion.

Camera Style: Early-2000s consumer DV camcorder aesthetic. Someone quietly recording from a short distance. Heavy handheld shake, imperfect framing, frequent autofocus hunting, lens breathing, exposure pumping between sun and shade, occasional motion blur, subtle rolling shutter, mild digital compression artifacts, faded colors, soft contrast, slight sensor noise. No stabilization. No cinematic camera moves. No modern color grading.

TIMED SCENES (00:00–00:15)

00:00–00:02 A sunlit street in a working-class Istanbul neighborhood, golden morning light filling the frame from the very first moment. Wide frontal shot from a distance, camera at chest height, looking straight down the street — the road stretching toward the camera, weathered apartment buildings rising on both sides, laundry lines and cables overhead, the street's depth fully visible. She appears as a figure further down the street, walking toward the camera standing between the two handles of her cart, gripping one in each hand, the tall white sack rolling behind her, her face in quiet focus. The camera struggles to hold focus on her as she slowly approaches and grows larger in the frame.

00:02–00:04 She sets the handles down beside a grey metal dumpster, lifts the curved lid, pulls out a tied black garbage bag and tears it open. She digs through the mixed household waste — packaging, cardboard, scraps — and finds one plastic bottle buried in it, shakes it off, and tosses it over into the white sack on her cart. Close on her face as she works; the autofocus hunts between her eyes and her moving hands.

00:04–00:06 She steps back between the handles, grips them at her sides, leans her whole body forward and pulls the heavy cart up a slight incline in the street — real physical effort in her back and legs, morning sun behind her. Exposure pumps as she moves between sunlight and building shade.

00:06–00:08 She sets the handles down, straightens up, presses her hands to her lower back and stretches with a quiet exhale, glancing back down the street she just climbed. She wipes her forehead with the back of her glove.

00:08–00:10 She pushes the collected bottles deeper into the white sack with both hands, settling the load. In the soft-focus background, two passersby slow down and turn their heads to look at her as they pass. She stays fully absorbed in the work, unaware of them. Loose handheld side angle with natural camera drift.

00:10–00:12 She suddenly notices the camera has been recording. She stops and looks directly into the lens — a long, steady, unreadable stare lasting the full two seconds. The camera pulls back slightly, unsettled. Then she simply returns to her work as if the camera doesn't matter.

00:12–00:15 She steps between the handles, lifts them, and pulls the cart toward the next dumpster further down the sunlit street, walking away from the camera in the same golden light the video opened with. The video ends mid-motion with a hard instant cut, the image simply stopping.

Audio: Natural ambient sound only — morning birds, distant city traffic, the rattle of metal cart wheels on pavement, the metal dumpster lid, a garbage bag tearing, a plastic bottle landing in the sack, her footsteps, fabric moving, distant neighborhood sounds, a far-off simit seller call. No music. No narration. She never speaks.

Goal: A real morning in the life of a young waste collector in Istanbul, captured like a forgotten home video from the early 2000s — raw, candid, dignified, unsentimental, and deeply believable. Striking beauty observed in an ordinary hard-working life, never performed.
```

On an escort gun-truck, a gunner swings his mounted gun around. Sound: roaring engines, screeching brakes, groaning metal, the alien's low roar, radio voices.

Shot 1 (0–3s) — THE HOOK: Low front shot on the road. The giant alien wraps its slimy tentacles around the lead fuel truck, the steel tank bending and creaking as the truck slews sideways across the highway. The other trucks brake hard, tires smoking. The lead driver fights the wheel and shouts into his radio: "IT'S CRUSHING MY TANK!"

Shot 2 (3–6s): On the escort gun-truck. A gunner swings his mounted gun toward the alien, but holds his fire — the driver still in the cab, the fuel tank wrapped tight. He calls on the radio: "Jump clear! Get off the road!"

Shot 3 (6–9s): At the lead truck. The driver throws his door open and leaps out, rolling clear onto the sand and running from the road. Shot 4 (9–13s) — BIG SLOW-MOTION MOMENT: The alien is out in the open on the road now, the fuel truck behind it. The gunner fires. The truck bursts — a huge fireball blooming around the alien.

Shot 5 (13–15s): Time snaps back. The whole fuel truck and the alien go up in one massive rolling fireball on the highway, fire and slime shooting into the sky. The other trucks reverse hard as the rescued driver runs clear across the sand.

[0:00–0:02] Shot 1 — Wide low-angle establishing shot
Ronin walks slowly through dense bamboo/jungle foliage, hand resting on sheathed katana. Two shadowed enemy figures visible flanking him in the background haze. Camera static with slow push-in. Ambient jungle sound, distant insect chirr.

[0:02–0:03.5] Shot 2 — Extreme close-up whip-pan to iai draw

[0:03.5–0:05] Shot 3 — Wide diagonal action shot

[0:05–0:06.5] Shot 4 — Over-the-shoulder orbit shot
Camera orbits behind Ronin as he pivots on one geta sandal, parrying enemy #2's incoming curved blade with the flat of his katana. Bandaged forearm braces the block. Dirt and leaves kick up around his feet.

[0:06.5–0:07.5] Shot 5 — Extreme close-up on face

[0:07.5–0:09.5] Shot 6 — Low-angle wide, slow-motion ramp

[0:09.5–0:11] Shot 7 — Dead-center symmetrical clash
Blade meets claw in mid-air, spark burst at point of contact, both figures frozen in impact for a beat. Faces inches apart. Bright spark flare, sudden silence after.

[0:11–0:13] Shot 8 — Wide low-angle, slow-motion follow

[0:13–0:15] Shot 9 — Wide static hold, aftermath
Ronin stands motionless among the fallen enemies, katana lowered at his side with blood dripping from the tip. Dust and loose straw settle in the light shafts. Kasa hat still shadows his eyes. Camera holds completely still — no movement, tension released, ambient jungle sound returns (wind, distant birdcall).

Maintain environment, lighting angle, and color grade from Image 2 across all 9 shots — same jungle, same time of day, no lighting jumps.

Aspect ratio: 16:9.

Create a hyper-cinematic 15-second continuous single-take extreme desert sandboarding video.

REFERENCE USAGE:
Image 1 is a strict character sheet reference. Preserve the woman’s exact identity, face, pale skin, blue eyes, long straight black hair, body proportions, desert outfit, tight short shorts, boots, scarf details, and sandboard design. The goggles must stay only on top of her head, never around her neck or over her eyes. Do not recreate the character sheet layout, text, labels, or multiple views. Show only one real woman performing the action.

SETTING:
A vast open desert in bright daylight, with giant rolling dunes, strong sunlight, blowing sand, drifting dust, and a raw cinematic atmosphere. The dune shapes should flow like ocean waves, but the surface is always dry desert sand, never water.

VIDEO STYLE:
One continuous 15-second shot, absolutely no cuts, no hidden cuts, no transitions, no montage. Hyper-realistic motion, natural motion blur, sand particles hitting the lens, board scraping over sand, rushing wind, and believable balance corrections. No text, no logos, no subtitles.

OPENING SHOT:
She is already sandboarding when the video begins. The camera starts very close in front of her upper body and face. Her hair moves in the wind, her expression is calm, fearless, and focused, and the sand flows rapidly beneath her board.

CAMERA MOVEMENT:
The camera starts in front of her, then smoothly swings around her body to the back. As soon as it reaches behind her, it drops low to sandboard level and stays there for the rest of the video. From that moment on, it remains very close to the board, low near the sand surface, following from behind and slightly offset near the tail. It must never rise to a high angle.

ACTION FLOW:
Once the camera settles low behind her, she drops into a deep crouch and accelerates hard across the dune face. She leans forward, shifts her weight with athletic precision, and reaches one hand down so her fingertips skim the sand, throwing particles backward toward the low camera.

She then carves fluid slalom turns along the dune curves. Her movement should feel smooth and wave-like, as if surfing across desert waves. She flows through several dune ridges with speed and control while the low camera stays locked close behind the board.

She builds more speed toward a sharp dune lip and launches into an acrobatic jump. The entire jump stays in the same continuous shot. During the airborne moment, time shifts into dramatic slow motion while the camera remains low, close, and near sandboard level, following from behind and slightly underneath. In the air, she turns her upper body toward the camera, looks directly into the lens, and holds strong direct eye contact with the viewer while grabbing the sandboard with one hand. Her hair and scarf lift in the wind, and sand particles hang in the air.

At the end of the jump, the slow motion must stop and return to normal speed. She lands hard and fast on the dune with a strong impact, a heavy burst of sand, and a sharp board-contact feeling. The landing must be clearly visible, powerful, rough, and satisfying. After landing, she keeps control and rides forward briefly in full normal speed.

FINAL MOMENT:
End right after the hard landing and short ride-out, with sand exploding behind the board and the camera still low and chasing from behind.
```

Early-2000s consumer DV camcorder.
Friend casually filming.

00:12–00:15

The camera trails several steps behind.
She suddenly realizes she is still being filmed.
She glances back over her shoulder.
A small genuine smile appears.
She looks away again and keeps walking.
The operator lowers the camcorder slightly.
Recording abruptly cuts to black mid-motion as if the camera was accidentally switched off.

Water dripping from roofs and leaves.
Footsteps on wet concrete.
Distant neighborhood traffic.
Occasional scooter passing far away.
Birds sheltering in trees.
Rustling leaves.
Faint residential chatter.
Cat sounds.
Subtle wind.
No music.
No narration.
No sound design.
No added effects.

Generation Priority
Authenticity > Beauty
Believability > Cinematics
Natural Imperfections > Technical Perfection

She wears an oversized off-white cotton T-shirt, loose faded blue jeans, classic black Converse-style sneakers, a simple silver necklace, and carries a beige canvas tote bag. Healthy natural skin texture, minimal makeup, calm personality, warm genuine smile. Maintain identical identity, clothing, hairstyle, and facial features throughout the entire video.

Location:
A peaceful residential neighborhood in Tokyo, Japan, Spring 2026, around 9:00 AM. Narrow spotless streets, compact modern homes, bicycles, utility poles, vending machines, tiny gardens, flowering plants, neighborhood cats, railway crossing, small roadside shrines, trimmed hedges, maple trees, laundry drying on balconies, soft morning sunlight. No tourists, no shopping districts, no advertisements, no crowds.

Visual Style:
Ultra-realistic documentary footage.
Authentic early-2000s MiniDV home camcorder aesthetic.
Heavy handheld movement.
Natural camera shake.
Slightly imperfect framing.
Frequent autofocus hunting.
Exposure changes between sunlight and shade.
Rolling shutter.
CCD sensor bloom.
Soft faded consumer-video colors.
Subtle analog tape noise.
Occasional MiniDV glitches.
Mild compression artifacts.
No stabilization.
No cinematic camera moves.
No HDR.
No modern color grading.
Everything should feel like a forgotten family recording.

00:00–00:02
She stops at a tiny unmanned roadside flower stand, places coins into an honesty payment box, picks up a small bouquet, smiles naturally, and continues walking.

00:02–00:04
She buys a canned iced coffee from a vending machine standing alone on a quiet residential street. The camera briefly loses focus before recovering.

00:04–00:06
An elderly neighbor quietly sweeps the public sidewalk outside several homes. She politely bows while passing, and the neighbor smiles back.

00:06–00:08
She waits patiently at a neighborhood railway crossing with a few local residents. A commuter train passes as sunlight flickers through the trees.

00:08–00:10
A neighborhood cat sleeps peacefully beside an unlocked bicycle. She crouches, smiles softly, but lets the cat sleep without touching it.

00:10–00:12
She walks beneath quiet residential streets carrying her coffee and flowers while a gentle breeze moves her hair and hanging wind chimes ring softly.

00:12–00:15
She reaches a small bridge overlooking a narrow canal with koi fish below. She pauses, looks toward the camera with a warm genuine smile, gives a small wave, then walks away as the MiniDV recording abruptly cuts to black with a brief analog tape glitch.

Audio:
Only natural environmental sounds—morning birds, soft wind, rustling leaves, distant commuter train, railway crossing bells, bicycle wheels, footsteps, neighborhood ambience, faint wind chimes, subtle camcorder handling noise. No music. No narration. No sound effects.

Goal:
Create a deeply believable slice of everyday Japanese life that feels peaceful, authentic, nostalgic, and emotionally comforting. Every movement should appear completely natural, making viewers feel like they are watching a real forgotten MiniDV recording of an ordinary morning in Japan.

Overall goal:

0至1秒 — 起势冲刺。低角度宽景镜头。两人已从聚落两端同时爆发冲刺,炎刃双刀出鞘带起暗红色能量残影,凯尔托利爪完全弹出金属声清脆,双方脚下沙尘随冲刺猛烈扬起。
1至2秒 — 首次碰撞。近特写撞击瞬间。炎刃与凯尔托正面相撞,刀刃与利爪碰撞迸发火花,冲击波将沙地炸开成圆形,两人未停顿借反震力立即错身再攻。
2至3秒 — 连续快攻。快速甩镜跟拍。凯尔托连续三击利爪连斩,炎刃格挡前两击,第三击擦过肩膀撕裂布料划出血迹,毫不迟疑立即反手回击。
3至4秒 — 低扫上斩。低角度追拍镜头。炎刃低身旋转以第二把刀低扫踝部高度,凯尔托跃起躲避,炎刃顺势向上斩击拖出暗红能量轨迹。
4至5秒 — 空中碰撞。极近特写慢动作。两人半空正面相撞,利爪刮过刀刃迸出火花,碰撞后立即借力翻身继续下压攻势。
5至6秒 — 落地再战。动态侧面镜头。落地瞬间立刻重新接战,凯尔托全力扑击,炎刃侧身闪过横斩其肋部划出血迹,凯尔托攻势不减反增。
6至7秒 — 强袭反制。极近特写。凯尔托抓住炎刃持刀手臂同时利爪刺入其侧腹,炎刃闷哼鲜血浸透衣料,另一手刀刃已反手扎向凯尔托肩膀。
7至8秒 — 挣脱连斩。快速跟拍镜头。炎刃头撞震退凯尔托,旋转双斩爆发暗色能量闪焰,凯尔托交叉利爪堪堪格挡被震得后滑。
8至9秒 — 环境崩塌。远景动态镜头。双斩轰裂古代石柱,碎石沙尘坠落,两人未曾后退,借碎石掩护欺身近战继续缠斗。
9至10秒 — 二次冲刺对攻。低角度双人跟拍。两人再度贴身近战,刀刃利爪连续交错碰撞五次以上,火花沙尘不间断迸发,节奏密集如鼓点。
10至11.5秒 — 终极蓄力碰撞。极限慢动作。两人同时挥出最大一击,刀刃被暗红能量缠绕,利爪泛橙光,正面对撞瞬间冲击力将沙地以圆形炸飞。
11.5至13秒 — 冲击波中连续追击。慢动作转常速。冲击波未散,两人借冲击力反弹继续贴身互搏,连续斩击与格挡毫无喘息之机。
13至14.5秒 — 最终交锋爆发。近特写快切。两人使出全力最后一击,刀刃利爪在画面中央狠狠相扣,暗红能量与部落橙光剧烈交织爆闪,火花如瀑布四溅。
14.5至15秒 — 硬切结束。极近特写。刀刃利爪仍紧扣对峙,能量爆闪达最亮瞬间,画面直接硬切黑场,无淡出无定格,战斗感在最高点戛然而止。

### A 15-second cinematic miniature chase across a warm afternoon living-room…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
A 15-second cinematic miniature chase across a warm afternoon living-room floor, ONE CONTINUOUS SHOT, NO CUTS. The entire video is one forward escape along the same wooden floor path. Every danger is a connected chain reaction on that path — not separate scenes.

Camera: Very low floor-level chase cam at the girl's shoulder height, tightly behind or slightly beside her. The camera never resets position, never jumps to a new angle, never teleports. It accelerates with her sprint, leans into her turns, dips when she slides, and shakes on near-misses. One continuous physical camera move from start to finish. Extremely shallow macro depth of field — she stays sharp, the giant world blurs in and out as it passes.

Chain of events: The camera starts low behind the tiny girl sprinting away from the desk area (Episode 1 landing point) across sunlit floorboards, dust motes drifting at her knee height like snowfall. — A shadow slides over her from behind: the cat has spotted her. Its paw-steps land as deep thuds. — She cuts hard around a fallen pencil; the cat's first paw slap lands inches behind her, flipping the pencil into a spin — she jumps the spinning pencil without breaking stride. — The pencil rolls into a stack of magazines, sliding the top one across the floor like a glacier into her path — she drops into a slide under its lifting edge, board-slide posture, and keeps running. — The magazine knocks a ball of yarn loose; it unspools rolling beside her, the thread whipping past her face — she vaults over the crossing thread. — The cat overtakes from the side in full "우다다" zoomies mode, cuts ahead, and crouches into pounce position directly on her path, hind legs wiggling — at the last second she veers into the dark gap under the sofa, sliding on her knees as the cat's paw sweeps the entrance behind her, claws retracted, batting at darkness. — Inside the dim under-sofa tunnel she keeps moving; behind her in the bright entrance slit, the cat's face drops sideways into view, one huge amber eye peering in, tail flicking. She backs away deeper into the dark, breathing hard — and the eye blinks slowly. Not menace — curiosity. The video ends on that suspended standoff, a split second of "safe... for now."

Cat behavior: playful hunting instinct, not evil — claws never extended, movements are classic house-cat play (slap, pounce-wiggle, zoomies, slow blink). The cat stays physically consistent — it moves through the room continuously, never teleports.

Audio: no background music for the first 3 seconds — only her sprinting footsteps (tiny, fast) and giant paw thuds (slow, deep, getting closer): the rhythm contrast IS the tension. Then urgent strings enter low. Realistic scale sounds: pencil clatter like a falling log, magazine sliding like an avalanche, yarn thread whipping, the cat's purr-chirp echoing huge, her breathing close to camera. At the sofa-gap escape all music cuts to silence — under the sofa: muffled room tone, her heartbeat, and the soft "mrrp?" of the curious cat. End on one slow-blink sound of a giant eyelid.

Style: photoreal live-action look, warm natural window light, tilt-shift miniature feel, consistent room scale and floor layout throughout. She stays ~12cm at all times; the cat stays giant. All action physically connected and continuous. No slow motion, no face morphing, no cuts of any kind.
```

Sound: low-frequency electronic swell building up, distant crowd cheering (off-screen).

[00:10-00:15] Shot 3: Drone Swarm Finale
Wide low-angle shot: thousands of drone lights converge from all directions in the city night sky, first forming a star pattern, then flowing and morphing into glowing text "1776 → 2026".
VFX: a final row of fireworks launches simultaneously from the horizon and bursts, golden ember rain filling the lower edge of the frame as the drone text freezes amid the firelight.
[ON-SCREEN TEXT] Golden serif title fades in at center frame: "250 YEARS", softly glowing, holds for 1 second to close.
Sound: orchestral score peaks then cuts abruptly, leaving only the echo of fireworks.
```

### Main subject: young Indian man, early 20s, lean athletic build, natural…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Main subject: young Indian man, early 20s, lean athletic build, natural everyday appearance, faded forest-green oversized t-shirt, dark charcoal cargo pants, worn trekking shoes, rugged canvas backpack, simple analog wristwatch, Realistic skin texture, no stylized features, calm introspective personality. Accompanied by his loyal medium-sized dog with short golden-brown fur, alert ears, expressive eyes, and a worn dark collar. Maintain consistent identity, clothing, dog appearance, gear, and physical characteristics throughout the entire video. 

Location: Authentic rural Kerala during monsoon season. Journey begins in a small roadside town market with old buses, rain-soaked roads, vegetable stalls, umbrellas, puddles, hanging signboards, motorcycles, and local commuters. The journey transitions into beautiful Kerala jungle roads with dense tropical vegetation, mountain roads, waterfalls along roadside cliffs, mist-covered valleys, heavy greenery, moss-covered rocks, and rolling clouds. Final location is a high mountain ridge surrounded by deep forest and expansive cloud-filled valleys.

Visual Style: Ultra-realistic documentary realism. Genuine candid behavior. Natural body language. Unscripted slice-of-life feeling. Strong environmental authenticity. Rich real-world details and believable human and animal motion. 

Camera Style: Early-2000s consumer DV camcorder aesthetic. Friend casually recording everyday moments. Heavy handheld shake, imperfect framing, frequent autofocus hunting, lens breathing, exposure pumping when moving between sun and shade, occasional motion blur, subtle rolling shutter, mild digital compression artifacts, faded colors, soft contrast, slight sensor noise. No stabilization. No cinematic camera moves. No modern color grading. 

00:00–00:02 Inside an old public bus leaving a rainy roadside market. He sits on the window seat with his dog beside him, front paws resting near the window. Rain droplets streak across the glass. He quietly looks outside while blurred market activity passes by. The camera struggles to hold focus as the bus shakes over uneven roads. 

00:02–00:04 The bus moves deeper into winding jungle roads lined with dense tropical greenery. The dog curiously watches outside with twitching ears. Framing drifts slightly off-center as the camera operator tries to capture both the scenery and their reactions. 

00:04–00:06 Through the bus window, mist covered hills, waterfalls, and wet mountain roads pass by. Autofocus repeatedly shifts between raindrops on the glass and his thoughtful face.

00:06–00:08 The bus stops near a remote roadside trail. Light monsoon rain continues. He steps down carrying his backpack while the dog jumps out excitedly beside him. 

00:08–00:10 They trek uphill through dense forest. Wet leaves brush against his clothes and shoes sink slightly into soft muddy ground. The dog runs ahead, occasionally turning back toward him. 

00:10–00:12 At the mountain summit, he sets up a small tent on damp ground while thick clouds drift through the valley below. Wind moves his shirt and the tent fabric. Loose handheld side angle with natural camera drift. 

00:12–00:15 Near the tent, a small grill crackles over open flame. He grills fresh fish and tears pieces to feed his dog before eating himself. Both sit quietly facing the vast valley and rolling clouds. He notices the camera, gives a small genuine smile, then looks back at the view. 

Audio: Natural ambient sound only bus engine vibrations, distant market chatter, rain hitting metal surfaces, windshield wipers, jungle insects, dripping leaves, wet footsteps on mud, dog breathing, tent fabric moving in wind, crackling grill fire, distant thunder, subtle mountain ambience. No music. No sound design. No narration. 

Goal: Authentic Kerala wilderness travel life captured like a forgotten home video from the early 2000s — candid, imperfect, realistic, warm, and deeply believable.
```

### Use an uploaded character sheet as an identity reference to keep a character…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use an uploaded character sheet as an identity reference to keep a character consistent (facial features, hairstyle, proportions, outfit colors/accessories) and explicitly add “no identity drift” to the prompt.  
Use an uploaded monster image as a design reference and list the exact traits to preserve (horned head, glowing red eyes, muscular body, multiple large tentacles).  
Lock a consistent anime look by specifying 2D anime cinematic animation, clean even-weight vector linework, flat 2–3 tone cel shading with hard shadow edges, grouped-clump hair motion, and painterly cool/desaturated backgrounds with atmospheric haze.  
Stabilize motion by prompting for smooth animation, stable camera, physically accurate movement, cloth simulation, hair simulation, and explicitly adding “no jitter” and “no flicker.”  
Prevent common artifacts by adding negative constraints like “no warped anatomy,” “no extra limbs,” and “no duplicated body parts.”  
Get controlled results by writing a scene-by-scene storyboard with shot types (wide establishing, low-angle, close-up), camera moves (slow follow, tilt up, slight circle), and timing cues (slow motion, brief glow then fade).  
Direct lighting and mood by describing specific lighting events (cinematic volumetric lighting, a narrow beam of sunlight isolating the character while the environment stays dark).  
Increase perceived impact by prompting for anime action effects tied to motion (heavy speed lines, dust trails, impact burst, shockwave, debris scatter).  
Maintain subject/environment separation by prompting that characters remain crisp against softer environments.  
Request high-detail output by specifying 4K quality and cinematic framing.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Use an uploaded character sheet as an identity reference to keep a character consistent (facial features, hairstyle, proportions, outfit colors/accessories) and explicitly add “no identity drift” to the prompt.  
Use an uploaded monster image as a design reference and list the exact traits to preserve (horned head, glowing red eyes, muscular body, multiple large tentacles).  
Lock a consistent anime look by specifying 2D anime cinematic animation, clean even-weight vector linework, flat 2–3 tone cel shading with hard shadow edges, grouped-clump hair motion, and painterly cool/desaturated backgrounds with atmospheric haze.  
Stabilize motion by prompting for smooth animation, stable camera, physically accurate movement, cloth simulation, hair simulation, and explicitly adding “no jitter” and “no flicker.”  
Prevent common artifacts by adding negative constraints like “no warped anatomy,” “no extra limbs,” and “no duplicated body parts.”  
Get controlled results by writing a scene-by-scene storyboard with shot types (wide establishing, low-angle, close-up), camera moves (slow follow, tilt up, slight circle), and timing cues (slow motion, brief glow then fade).  
Direct lighting and mood by describing specific lighting events (cinematic volumetric lighting, a narrow beam of sunlight isolating the character while the environment stays dark).  
Increase perceived impact by prompting for anime action effects tied to motion (heavy speed lines, dust trails, impact burst, shockwave, debris scatter).  
Maintain subject/environment separation by prompting that characters remain crisp against softer environments.  
Request high-detail output by specifying 4K quality and cinematic framing.

### 参照画像を使って「同一人物維持」を明示し、顔・髪型・衣装・装飾・体格・武器を全セクションで完全統一して別人化や衣装変更を防ぐ

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
参照画像を使って「同一人物維持」を明示し、顔・髪型・衣装・装飾・体格・武器を全セクションで完全統一して別人化や衣装変更を防ぐ  
16:9指定で、左35%にメイン全身・右65%に資料パネルを配置する「キャラクタービジュアルシート（presentation board）」構図をプロンプトで指示して情報量の多い設定資料風に仕上げる  
MODEL VIEW（正面図＋背面図）を明記して、髪の長さ・ドレスのシルエット・背面リボン等の構造が読めるように全身の前後を同条件で描かせる  
EXPRESSIONSを2種類（通常・目を閉じた穏やか）と指定し、顔比率・瞳・輪郭・髪飾りの統一をプロンプトで固定して表情差分を作る  
BATTLE SNAPSHOTSを横長2枚（抜刀直前の低い構え／高速横薙ぎ＋サファイアブルーの斬撃光）と具体的に指示し、動きが速くても顔と刀の軌道が読めることを条件に入れる  
WEAPON CLOSE-UPで日本刀と鞘の横向きディテール（漆塗り・組紐・宝石・金属装飾・チェーン・房飾り）を列挙して工芸品レベルの精密描写を狙う  
PALETTE & MATERIALを六角形/宝石型サンプルで横一列に並べ、髪・瞳・レース・サテン等の素材ごとに質感と反射が分かるよう指定して配色とマテリアルを統一する
```

#### 💡 Dicas

参照画像を使って「同一人物維持」を明示し、顔・髪型・衣装・装飾・体格・武器を全セクションで完全統一して別人化や衣装変更を防ぐ

### Storyboard your Seedance 2.0 video as timestamped shot prompts (e.g., 0:01–0:02…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Storyboard your Seedance 2.0 video as timestamped shot prompts (e.g., 0:01–0:02 low-angle tracking shot; 0:02–0:04 close-up unboxing; 0:04–0:07 top-down flat-lay; 0:07–0:09 extreme macro detail; 0:18–0:24 vertical pan to mirror selfie) to control pacing and camera language across a 20-second commercial.

Use explicit camera-direction keywords in each prompt (low-angle tracking shot, close-up, flat-lay top-down, extreme close-up macro, side-angle close-up, vertical pan) to steer composition and motion.

Add product-specific material and detail descriptors (olive green suede, silver hardware emblem, red/silver charm, off-white rubber sole) to keep the footwear design consistent across shots.

Anchor scenes with consistent environment/style cues (bright sunlit room, white slatted flooring, polished white marble floor, minimalist pottery, warm polished stone floor) to maintain a cohesive luxury aesthetic across cuts.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

### Structure your Seedance prompt as a multi-cut storyboard with timestamps (e.g…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Structure your Seedance prompt as a multi-cut storyboard with timestamps (e.g., CUT 1 0–3s … CUT 5 12–15s) to control pacing across a 15s video  
Lock character consistency by listing each character’s fixed appearance traits (hair, eyes, outfit, personality) under a Characters section and keeping them unchanged across all cuts  
Specify cinematic horror lighting and atmosphere directly in the style prompt (realistic lighting, volumetric fog, flashlight lighting, dark atmosphere) to drive mood and scene readability  
Control camera behavior per cut with explicit directions (slow handheld tracking shot, slow pan, slow zoom, fast handheld) to shape tension and energy  
Add horror beats as concrete scene actions (flashlights flicker → near darkness → reveal ghost nurse → jump behind characters) to create clear visual transitions between cuts  
Include on-screen text and ending instructions (fade to black + final caption) to force a cinematic horror ending within the last seconds of the clip
```

#### 💡 Dicas

### Specify a camera lens in the reference frame prompt (e.g., 35mm vs 85mm) to…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Specify a camera lens in the reference frame prompt (e.g., 35mm vs 85mm) to control depth and reduce distortion during pans/dollies.  
Describe lighting with direction and color temperature (e.g., key light from camera-left, 5600K) instead of just saying cinematic to prevent shadow/lighting artifacts in motion.  
Set an explicit shot type (wide/medium/macro) in the reference frame prompt to constrain framing and reduce drift across the clip.  
Use a single anchor mood word to stabilize color grading across frames instead of a long list of adjectives that can cause grade shifts.  
Maintain environment continuity by keeping landmarks and light sources consistent in the reference frame prompt to prevent background swaps mid-motion.  
Include a specific cinematic reference (director or film style) in the reference frame prompt to avoid generic staging and lock the visual language faster.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Set an explicit shot type (wide/medium/macro) in the reference frame prompt to constrain framing and reduce drift across the clip.  
Use a single anchor mood word to stabilize color grading across frames instead of a long list of adjectives that can cause grade shifts.

### Build a full character sheet (not a single portrait) and use it as the hard…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Build a full character sheet (not a single portrait) and use it as the hard character reference in Seedance 2.0 to prevent identity drift across multiple scenes.

Write a locked character bible before generating any video (name, body build, uniform, bib number, brand colors) and keep these details fixed for the entire sequence.

Create a single turnaround sheet with multiple angles (front, 3/4 front, side, 3/4 back, back) to anchor the character’s geometry for Seedance.

Include an expression sheet (focused, exertion, post-finish, victory, pre-race) so Seedance has a predefined emotional range to pull from.

Add action poses (full sprint, steady run, warm up, finish line) to encode movement mechanics so Seedance animates the correct body behavior per scene.

Lock costume details with exact hex color codes (fabric, shoes, accessories, brand palette) to keep uniform colors consistent between shots.

Stitch/generated clips in narrative order (start line → finish victory) to maintain continuity across the race sequence.

If a specific expression fails in close-up, regenerate that shot (e.g., redo the victory face) rather than changing the character reference.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Write a locked character bible before generating any video (name, body build, uniform, bib number, brand colors) and keep these details fixed for the entire sequence.

Create a single turnaround sheet with multiple angles (front, 3/4 front, side, 3/4 back, back) to anchor the character’s geometry for Seedance.

If a specific expression fails in close-up, regenerate that shot (e.g., redo the victory face) rather than changing the character reference.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use ChatGPT Image 2.0 to generate a detailed storyboard (including asset layout and interior details), then feed the resulting static frame into Seedance 2.0 as an image reference to render a seamless continuous shot with no cuts.

In Dreamina, select Video → Seedance 2.0 and enable JSON mode (Normal/JSON toggle) to control generations via structured JSON prompts.

For Seedance 2.0 image-to-video, upload the corresponding storyboard frame as the reference image, paste/validate your JSON prompt, and run a test generation first before iterating on the JSON for final output.

Set Seedance 2.0 duration to 5 seconds for regular scenes and 8–10 seconds for transformation shots.

Generate storyboard frames one-by-one in ChatGPT Image 2.0, save them sequentially (Scene_01.jpg, Scene_02.jpg, etc.), and review them as a slideshow before starting Seedance video generation.

Create and use a character sheet in ChatGPT Image 2.0 before storyboarding to keep characters consistent across frames.

In editing, trim the first and last 0.5 seconds off each generated clip to remove unstable frames.

Use Cross Dissolve for smooth transitions and Hard Cut for sharp transitions when assembling Seedance clips into an edit.
```

### Generate a 4x2 (8-panel) 16:9 storyboard sheet first, with panel headers…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Generate a 4x2 (8-panel) 16:9 storyboard sheet first, with panel headers P01–P08 and fixed camera angle/shot scale/framing/screen direction per beat, then use that storyboard as the shot-for-shot blueprint for Seedance 2.0.

In the Seedance prompt, explicitly tell Seedance to ignore storyboard-only elements (clay-previs style, blank mannequins, borders, stray marks) while preserving the mapped layout/staging from the storyboard.

Lock character identity by assigning separate references and stating that @[char1] is the sole source for C1’s final appearance and @[char2] is the sole source for C2’s final appearance (references affect appearance only, not layout/staging).

Maintain continuity by specifying consistent environment geography and screen direction across all beats (e.g., C1 generally screen left moving screen right; C2 screen right resisting left) and enforcing exactly two fighter bodies per shot (with any extra “spirit” as an effect, not a third character).

Control pacing by instructing hard cuts through P01–P06, a single speed ramp/slow moment at P07, and a held release at P08.

Prevent unwanted overlays by restricting visible text to only the two header lines and panel headers, and explicitly banning in-panel text, labels, arrows, icons, callouts, and extra panels.

Keep VFX physically grounded by specifying limb-attached aura trails and impact-attached events (contact bursts, shockwave rings, dust rings, one spirit silhouette at P07) and forbidding effects that read as props or floating objects.

Specify audio constraints directly (no background music; only breaths/foley; one thunderclap at P07; then hard silence) to shape the final video’s sound design.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Block out your scene in 3D, record a real cinema camera move, then render it through Seedance 2.0 to transfer the exact camera trajectory, timing, and framing.

Generate a ~15-second “clay pass” of the blocked 3D scene with the recorded camera move and use it as the control/reference so Seedance 2.0 follows the shot like a real DP-directed camera.

### Generate the full scene’s audio first with seed-audio-1.0 (dialogue + SFX + BGM…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Generate the full scene’s audio first with seed-audio-1.0 (dialogue + SFX + BGM in one track), then feed that audio into Seedance 2.0 reference-to-video so the audio becomes the timing/narrative anchor and you can use a short natural video prompt instead of a second-by-second mega-prompt.  
Write Seed-Audio prompts in narrative order (timeline order), because whatever appears first in the text will sound first in the generated track.  
Prefix background music with an explicit label like Soundtrack: or background music to make it persist under the whole clip instead of being treated like a short sound effect.  
To sync an SFX during a spoken line, don’t imply simultaneity—split the dialogue and insert the SFX between the halves, or explicitly write “at the same time/as,” otherwise the model will render them sequentially.  
For each Seedance 2.0 shot, use exactly three inputs: one reference image (any frame to define look), one freshly generated reference audio track (to define timing), and one short natural-language prompt (mood/setting/roles).  
Avoid guessing shot length by setting Seedance video duration to match the generated audio length once the sound is locked.  
Use Seed-Audio TA2A voice referencing for consistent characters: feed up to 3 reference clips and tag them @audio1/@audio2 to assign which voice speaks which lines (≈30 seconds of recording is enough as a reference).  
Pin a character’s voice across shots by reusing the generated voice from an earlier shot as a reference and tagging that speaker as @audio1 (voice registration) to prevent voice drift.  
For cinematic look + character consistency, generate base frames with youchuan/v8.1 (Midjourney look), then use nano-banana-2 edit mode to swap in a consistent face from a character reference image, adding “strictly preserve the same lighting” to the edit prompt.  
In Seedance 2.0 R2V prompts, explicitly call out audio-driven sync points (e.g., “At the exact moment the steam hisses, he turns/gestures”) so visual actions land on specific sounds without writing timestamps.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Generate the full scene’s audio first with seed-audio-1.0 (dialogue + SFX + BGM in one track), then feed that audio into Seedance 2.0 reference-to-video so the audio becomes the timing/narrative anchor and you can use a short natural video prompt instead of a second-by-second mega-prompt.  
Write Seed-Audio prompts in narrative order (timeline order), because whatever appears first in the text will sound first in the generated track.  
Prefix background music with an explicit label like Soundtrack: or background music to make it persist under the whole clip instead of being treated like a short sound effect.  
To sync an SFX during a spoken line, don’t imply simultaneity—split the dialogue and insert the SFX between the halves, or explicitly write “at the same time/as,” otherwise the model will render them sequentially.  
For each Seedance 2.0 shot, use exactly three inputs: one reference image (any frame to define look), one freshly generated reference audio track (to define timing), and one short natural-language prompt (mood/setting/roles).  
Avoid guessing shot length by setting Seedance video duration to match the generated audio length once the sound is locked.  
Use Seed-Audio TA2A voice referencing for consistent characters: feed up to 3 reference clips and tag them @audio1/@audio2 to assign which voice speaks which lines (≈30 seconds of recording is enough as a reference).

For cinematic look + character consistency, generate base frames with youchuan/v8.1 (Midjourney look), then use nano-banana-2 edit mode to swap in a consistent face from a character reference image, adding “strictly preserve the same lighting” to the edit prompt.  
In Seedance 2.0 R2V prompts, explicitly call out audio-driven sync points (e.g., “At the exact moment the steam hisses, he turns/gestures”) so visual actions land on specific sounds without writing timestamps.

### Use an edited reference image as the exact first frame and environment…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use an edited reference image as the exact first frame and environment reference to lock the skyline/road geometry and overall scene layout.  
Draw a guide line on the reference image to define the camera flight path, and explicitly instruct Seedance to follow it while also stating “Do not show the green line in the final video.”  
Specify a single continuous FPV drone shot with constraints like smooth stabilized flight, realistic banking turns, believable acceleration/inertia, and “no teleporting, no abrupt cuts, no impossible passes through buildings.”  
Control motion and story by writing a timestamped timeline (e.g., 0:00–0:03, 0:03–0:06, etc.) that describes exact camera moves and what must be visible in each segment.  
Increase realism by adding grounded environmental details tied to the reference (heavy monsoon rain, rain haze, wet reflective roads, grey storm clouds, cool blue-grey morning light, tiny moving traffic).  
Prevent unwanted overlays by explicitly banning text/captions/logos/watermarks in the prompt.  
If generating with audio, specify “No voiceover. No dialogue.” and list desired SFX layers (heavy rainfall, wind, distant thunder, drone motor whirr, faint traffic hum, sea movement) plus subtle tension music.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

### ギター/ドラムなど演奏のモーションコントロールはSeedance2.0でもカメラワーク付きである程度追従しつつディテールが溶けにくいので、演奏シーンに使う

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
ギター/ドラムなど演奏のモーションコントロールはSeedance2.0でもカメラワーク付きである程度追従しつつディテールが溶けにくいので、演奏シーンに使う  
演奏の動きの忠実さを最優先するシーンはKling Motion Controlを使い、カメラワークは控えめにして楽器と手の位置ズレを避ける  
カメラワークもある程度入れたいが演奏追従は多少妥協できるシーンはKling 3.0 Omniを使い、溶けやすさに注意して適用する  
MV制作では「バンド全体が映るシーン＝Seedance2.0」「ギター演奏/歌唱などモーション適用シーン＝Kling中心」のように、シーンごとにモデルを意図的に使い分ける  
演奏以外のシーンも混在するMV構成では、演奏パートのカメラワークを控えめにして演奏シンクを優先する  
LIVE演出のようにカメラを大きく動かしつつ演奏もシンクさせたい場合は、制作をほぼSeedance2.0に寄せる
```

#### 💡 Dicas

MV制作では「バンド全体が映るシーン＝Seedance2.0」「ギター演奏/歌唱などモーション適用シーン＝Kling中心」のように、シーンごとにモデルを意図的に使い分ける  
演奏以外のシーンも混在するMV構成では、演奏パートのカメラワークを控えめにして演奏シンクを優先する
